PROBLEM
------------------------------
I am playing with a set of objects. Here are the actions I can do

   Attack object
   Feast object from another object
   Succumb object
   Overcome object from another object

I have the following restrictions on my actions:
    To perform Attack action, the following facts need to be true: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.
    Once Attack action is performed the following facts will be true: Pain object.
    Once Attack action is performed the following facts will be false: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.
    To perform Succumb action, the following facts need to be true: Pain object.
    Once Succumb action is performed the following facts will be true: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.    
    Once Succumb action is performed the following facts will be false: Pain object.
    To perform Overcome action, the following needs to be true: Province other object, Pain object.
    Once Overcome action is performed the following will be true: Harmony, Province object, Object Craves other object.
    Once Overcome action is performed the following will be false: Province other object, Pain object.
    To perform Feast action, the following needs to be true: Object Craves other object, Province object, Harmony.
    Once Feast action is performed the following will be true: Pain object, Province other object.
    Once Feast action is performed the following will be false:, Object Craves other object, Province object, Harmony.

[STATEMENT]
As initial conditions I have that, object a craves object b, object d craves object c, harmony, planet object b, planet object c, province object a and province object d.
My goal is to have that object c craves object a.

My plan is as follows:

[PLAN]
================================================================================


ENVIRONMENT
------------------------------
I am playing with a set of objects. Here are the actions I can do

   Attack object
   Feast object from another object
   Succumb object
   Overcome object from another object

I have the following restrictions on my actions:
    To perform Attack action, the following facts need to be true: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.
    Once Attack action is performed the following facts will be true: Pain object.
    Once Attack action is performed the following facts will be false: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.
    To perform Succumb action, the following facts need to be true: Pain object.
    Once Succumb action is performed the following facts will be true: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.    
    Once Succumb action is performed the following facts will be false: Pain object.
    To perform Overcome action, the following needs to be true: Province other object, Pain object.
    Once Overcome action is performed the following will be true: Harmony, Province object, Object Craves other object.
    Once Overcome action is performed the following will be false: Province other object, Pain object.
    To perform Feast action, the following needs to be true: Object Craves other object, Province object, Harmony.
    Once Feast action is performed the following will be true: Pain object, Province other object.
    Once Feast action is performed the following will be false:, Object Craves other object, Province object, Harmony.

[STATEMENT]

FINAL-PLAN
------------------------------
<domain>
(define (domain jack_of_all_trades)
  (:requirements :strips :typing)
  (:types entity)
  (:predicates
    (province ?e - entity)
    (planet ?e - entity)
    (pain ?e - entity)
    (craves ?e1 ?e2 - entity)
    (harmony)
  )

  (:action attack
    :parameters (?obj - entity)
    :precondition (and (province ?obj) (planet ?obj) (harmony))
    :effect (and (pain ?obj) (not (province ?obj)) (not (planet ?obj)) (not (harmony)))
  )

  (:action succumb
    :parameters (?obj - entity)
    :precondition (pain ?obj)
    :effect (and (province ?obj) (planet ?obj) (harmony) (not (pain ?obj)))
  )

  (:action overcome
    :parameters (?obj - entity ?other - entity)
    :precondition (and (province ?other) (pain ?obj))
    :effect (and (harmony) (province ?obj) (craves ?obj ?other) (not (province ?other)) (not (pain ?obj)))
  )

  (:action feast
    :parameters (?obj - entity ?other - entity)
    :precondition (and (craves ?obj ?other) (province ?obj) (harmony))
    :effect (and (pain ?obj) (province ?other) (not (craves ?obj ?other)) (not (province ?obj)) (not (harmony)))
  )
)
</domain>
<problem>
(define (problem jack_of_all_trades_problem)
  (:domain jack_of_all_trades)
  (:objects
    a b c d - entity
  )
  (:init
    (craves a b)
    (craves d c)
    (harmony)
    (planet b)
    (planet c)
    (province a)
    (province d)
  )
  (:goal
    (and
      (craves c a)
    )
  )
)
</problem>

DOMAIN
------------------------------
(define (domain jack_of_all_trades)
  (:requirements :strips :typing)
  (:types entity)
  (:predicates
    (province ?e - entity)
    (planet ?e - entity)
    (pain ?e - entity)
    (craves ?e1 ?e2 - entity)
    (harmony)
  )

  (:action attack
    :parameters (?obj - entity)
    :precondition (and (province ?obj) (planet ?obj) (harmony))
    :effect (and (pain ?obj) (not (province ?obj)) (not (planet ?obj)) (not (harmony)))
  )

  (:action succumb
    :parameters (?obj - entity)
    :precondition (pain ?obj)
    :effect (and (province ?obj) (planet ?obj) (harmony) (not (pain ?obj)))
  )

  (:action overcome
    :parameters (?obj - entity ?other - entity)
    :precondition (and (province ?other) (pain ?obj))
    :effect (and (harmony) (province ?obj) (craves ?obj ?other) (not (province ?other)) (not (pain ?obj)))
  )

  (:action feast
    :parameters (?obj - entity ?other - entity)
    :precondition (and (craves ?obj ?other) (province ?obj) (harmony))
    :effect (and (pain ?obj) (province ?other) (not (craves ?obj ?other)) (not (province ?obj)) (not (harmony)))
  )
)
================================================================================


PROBLEM
------------------------------
(define (problem jack_of_all_trades_problem)
  (:domain jack_of_all_trades)
  (:objects
    a b c d - entity
  )
  (:init
    (craves a b)
    (craves d c)
    (harmony)
    (planet b)
    (planet c)
    (province a)
    (province d)
  )
  (:goal
    (and
      (craves c a)
    )
  )
)

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_0
------------------------------
Based on the provided PDDL domain, problem, and plan, here is the sequence of actions to achieve the goal where **object c craves object a**:

**Step 1: Feast object d from object c**
*   **Timestamp:** 00:00
*   **Action:** Object **d** performs a feast action involving object **c**. 
*   **Preconditions:** Object **d** must crave object **c**, object **d** must be in a province, and harmony must exist.
*   **Results:** Object **d** no longer craves object **c**, harmony is lost, and object **d** is no longer in its province. Object **d** now feels pain, and object **c** is established within a province.

**Step 2: Succumb object d**
*   **Timestamp:** 00:01
*   **Action:** Object **d** performs a succumb action.
*   **Preconditions:** Object **d** must be in a state of pain.
*   **Results:** Object **d**'s pain is removed. Harmony is restored, and object **d** is now designated as both a planet and part of a province.

**Step 3: Attack object c**
*   **Timestamp:** 00:02
*   **Action:** Perform an attack on object **c**.
*   **Preconditions:** Object **c** must be in a province, be a planet, and harmony must exist.
*   **Results:** Object **c** is no longer in a province, is no longer a planet, and harmony is lost. Object **c** now feels pain.

**Step 4: Overcome object c from object a**
*   **Timestamp:** 00:03
*   **Action:** Object **c** performs an overcome action involving object **a**.
*   **Preconditions:** Object **a** must be in a province and object **c** must feel pain.
*   **Results:** Object **c**'s pain is removed. Harmony is restored, and object **c** is now in a province. Crucially, **object c now craves object a**, satisfying the final goal. Object **a** is no longer in its province.

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1
------------------------------
Based on the provided plan, here is the sequence of human-readable instructions to achieve the goal:

**Action Sequence:**

1.  **00:00 - 00:01: Feast object b from object c**  
    Begin by feasting on object **b** from object **c**. This action is possible because object **b** craves **c**, object **b** is currently in the province, and there is harmony.

2.  **00:01 - 00:02: Succumb object b**  
    Next, succumb object **b**. This will alleviate the pain caused by the previous action and restore the province status, planet status, and harmony to the environment.

3.  **00:02 - 00:03: Feast object c from object d**  
    Feast on object **c** from object **d**. Since object **c** craves **d**, and harmony was restored in the previous step, this action allows the transition of the province to object **d**.

4.  **00:03 - 00:04: Succumb object c**  
    Succumb object **c** to restore the necessary environmental balances (province, planet, and harmony) and end the state of pain for object **c**.

5.  **00:04 - 00:05: Feast object d from object a**  
    Feast on object **d** from object **a**. This utilizes the fact that object **d** craves **a** and moves the focus of the province toward object **a**.

6.  **00:05 - 00:06: Succumb object d**  
    Succumb object **d** to once again restore the harmony and ground state of the environment.

7.  **00:06 - 00:07: Attack object a**  
    Attack object **a**. This action is valid now because **a** is a planet and currently in the province, and harmony exists. This results in pain for object **a** and removes harmony.

8.  **00:07 - 00:08: Overcome object a from object c**  
    Overcome object **a** using object **c**. This resolves the pain of object **a**, restores harmony, and establishes that object **a** now craves object **c** (fulfilling the first goal condition).

9.  **00:08 - 00:09: Attack object d**  
    Attack object **d**. This action is performed to put object **d** into a state of pain, which is a prerequisite for the final step.

10. **00:09 - 00:10: Overcome object d from object a**  
    Finally, overcome object **d** from object **a**. This restores the province to object **d** and establishes that object **d** craves object **a** (fulfilling the second goal condition).

**Goal Achievement:**  
At the conclusion of these steps, the requirements are satisfied: object **a** now craves object **c**, and object **d** craves object **a**.

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1
------------------------------
Based on the provided PDDL plan and domain specification, here is the sequence of actions to achieve the goal:

**Plan Overview:**
The objective is to manipulate the relationships between objects **a**, **b**, **c**, and **d** so that **object a craves object d** and **object d craves object b**. This is achieved through a series of "Feast," "Succumb," "Overcome," and "Attack" actions.

**Step-by-Step Instructions:**

1.  **00:00: Feast on object a from object d**
    *   Action: `feast d a`
    *   Effect: Object d no longer craves object a. Object d enters a state of pain, and object a becomes a province. Harmony is lost.

2.  **00:01: Succumb object d**
    *   Action: `succumb d`
    *   Effect: Object d recovers from pain. Object d becomes both a province and a planet, and harmony is restored.

3.  **00:02: Feast on object c from object a**
    *   Action: `feast a c`
    *   Effect: Object a no longer craves object c. Object a enters a state of pain, and object c becomes a province. Harmony is lost.

4.  **00:03: Overcome object a from object d**
    *   Action: `overcome a d`
    *   Effect: Object a overcomes its pain using province d. Object a now craves object d and becomes a province itself. Harmony is restored.

5.  **00:04: Feast on object b from object c**
    *   Action: `feast c b`
    *   Effect: Object c no longer craves object b. Object c enters a state of pain, and object b becomes a province. Harmony is lost.

6.  **00:05: Succumb object c**
    *   Action: `succumb c`
    *   Effect: Object c recovers from pain. Object c becomes a province and a planet, and harmony is restored.

7.  **00:06: Feast on object d from object a**
    *   Action: `feast a d`
    *   Effect: Object a no longer craves object d. Object a enters a state of pain, and object d becomes a province. Harmony is lost.

8.  **00:07: Succumb object a**
    *   Action: `succumb a`
    *   Effect: Object a recovers from pain. Object a becomes a province and a planet, and harmony is restored.

9.  **00:08: Attack object d**
    *   Action: `attack d`
    *   Effect: Object d is attacked. Object d enters a state of pain and is no longer a province or a planet. Harmony is lost.

10. **00:09: Overcome object d from object b**
    *   Action: `overcome d b`
    *   Effect: Object d overcomes its pain using province b. Object d now craves object b and becomes a province. Harmony is restored.

11. **00:10: Attack object a**
    *   Action: `attack a`
    *   Effect: Object a is attacked. Object a enters a state of pain and is no longer a province or a planet. Harmony is lost.

12. **00:11: Overcome object a from object d**
    *   Action: `overcome a d`
    *   Effect: Object a overcomes its pain using province d. Object a now craves object d and becomes a province. Harmony is restored.

**Final Goal State:**
*   Object a craves object d.
*   Object d craves object b.
The sequence is complete.

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1
------------------------------
Based on the provided PDDL domain, problem, and plan, here is the sequence of actions to achieve the goal:

**Initial State Summary:**
*   Object **b** craves object **a**.
*   Object **c** craves object **b**.
*   **Harmony** exists.
*   Objects **a** and **d** are identified as **planets**.
*   Objects **c** and **d** are located in a **province**.

**Action Sequence:**

1.  **Step 1: Attack object d**
    *   **Timestamp:** 00:00 - 00:01
    *   **Description:** Perform an attack on object **d**. 
    *   **Preconditions satisfied:** Object **d** is in a province, object **d** is a planet, and there is harmony.
    *   **Result:** Object **d** is now in pain. As a consequence, object **d** is no longer considered a planet or in a province, and harmony is lost.

2.  **Step 2: Overcome object d from object c**
    *   **Timestamp:** 00:01 - 00:02
    *   **Description:** Use object **d** to overcome object **c**.
    *   **Preconditions satisfied:** Object **c** is in a province and object **d** is in pain.
    *   **Result:** Object **d** now craves object **c**. Harmony is restored, object **d** is moved into a province, and object **c** is no longer in a province. Object **d** is no longer in pain.

**Final Goal State:**
*   Object **b** craves object **a** (maintained from the start).
*   Object **d** craves object **c** (achieved).
*   Goal conditions met.

PROBLEM
------------------------------
I am playing with a set of objects. Here are the actions I can do

   Attack object
   Feast object from another object
   Succumb object
   Overcome object from another object

I have the following restrictions on my actions:
    To perform Attack action, the following facts need to be true: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.
    Once Attack action is performed the following facts will be true: Pain object.
    Once Attack action is performed the following facts will be false: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.
    To perform Succumb action, the following facts need to be true: Pain object.
    Once Succumb action is performed the following facts will be true: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.    
    Once Succumb action is performed the following facts will be false: Pain object.
    To perform Overcome action, the following needs to be true: Province other object, Pain object.
    Once Overcome action is performed the following will be true: Harmony, Province object, Object Craves other object.
    Once Overcome action is performed the following will be false: Province other object, Pain object.
    To perform Feast action, the following needs to be true: Object Craves other object, Province object, Harmony.
    Once Feast action is performed the following will be true: Pain object, Province other object.
    Once Feast action is performed the following will be false:, Object Craves other object, Province object, Harmony.

[STATEMENT]
As initial conditions I have that, object a craves object c, object c craves object b, object d craves object a, harmony, planet object b and province object d.
My goal is to have that object a craves object b and object d craves object c.

    Once Succumb action is performed the following facts will be true: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.

    Once Feast action is performed the following will be true: Pain object, Province other object.
    Once Feast action is performed the following will be false:, Object Craves other object, Province object, Harmony.

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1
------------------------------
Based on the provided PDDL domain, problem, and plan, here is the sequence of human-readable actions required to achieve the goal:

**Plan Execution Sequence:**

*   **Step 1: 00:00 - Feast object d from object a.**
    *   This action uses object d’s province status and the fact that object d craves object a to transfer the province to object a. This results in object d being in pain and object a gaining the province status.

*   **Step 2: 00:01 - Succumb object d.**
    *   Object d yields, transforming its state of pain back into a state of harmony. This restores the province and planet status to object d.

*   **Step 3: 00:02 - Feast object a from object c.**
    *   With the harmony restored and object a holding the province, it feasts on object c (which it craves). Object a enters a state of pain, and object c now holds the province.

*   **Step 4: 00:03 - Succumb object a.**
    *   Object a yields, ending its pain and restoring harmony. This also grants object a the status of both a province and a planet.

*   **Step 5: 00:04 - Feast object c from object b.**
    *   Object c (the current province holder) feasts on object b (which it craves). Object c enters pain, and object b becomes a province.

*   **Step 6: 00:05 - Succumb object c.**
    *   Object c yields, ending its pain and restoring harmony. Object c now gains province and planet status.

*   **Step 7: 00:06 - Attack object a.**
    *   Because object a is currently both a province and a planet and there is harmony, an attack is performed on object a. This results in object a entering a state of pain and the loss of harmony.

*   **Step 8: 00:07 - Overcome object a from object b.**
    *   Utilizing the province status of object b and the pain of object a, object a overcomes object b. This restores harmony and establishes the goal that **object a now craves object b**.

*   **Step 9: 00:08 - Attack object d.**
    *   With harmony restored and object d having the necessary province and planet status, an attack is performed on object d. Object d enters a state of pain, and harmony is temporarily lost.

*   **Step 10: 00:09 - Overcome object d from object c.**
    *   Finally, using the province status of object c and the pain of object d, object d overcomes object c. This restores harmony and establishes the final goal that **object d now craves object c**.

**Final Goal Status:** Object a craves object b and object d craves object c. The plan is complete.

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1
------------------------------
To achieve the goal of having object **a** crave object **c**, the following sequence of actions must be performed. Each action transitions the state of the objects and the environment according to the specified rules.

**Step 1: Feast on object d using object a**
*   **Action:** `(feast a d)`
*   **Time:** 00:00 - 00:01
*   **Description:** Object **a** feasts on object **d**. This requires object **a** to crave **d**, **a** to be a province, and the presence of harmony. As a result, object **a** is now in pain, **d** becomes the new province, the craving between **a** and **d** is severed, and harmony is lost.

**Step 2: Object a succumbs**
*   **Action:** `(succumb a)`
*   **Time:** 00:01 - 00:02
*   **Description:** Object **a** succumbs to its pain. This requires object **a** to be in pain. Once performed, object **a** becomes both a province and a planet, harmony is restored to the environment, and the pain in object **a** is removed.

**Step 3: Feast on object b using object d**
*   **Action:** `(feast d b)`
*   **Time:** 00:02 - 00:03
*   **Description:** Object **d** feasts on object **b**. Since **d** became a province in Step 1 and harmony was restored in Step 2, this action can proceed. As a result, object **d** is now in pain, **b** becomes the new province, the craving between **d** and **b** is severed, and harmony is lost again.

**Step 4: Object d succumbs**
*   **Action:** `(succumb d)`
*   **Time:** 00:03 - 00:04
*   **Description:** Object **d** succumbs to its pain. Once performed, object **d** becomes a province and a planet, harmony is restored, and the pain in object **d** is removed.

**Step 5: Feast on object c using object b**
*   **Action:** `(feast b c)`
*   **Time:** 00:04 - 00:05
*   **Description:** Object **b** feasts on object **c**. Since **b** became a province in Step 3 and harmony was restored in Step 4, this action is valid. Consequently, object **b** is now in pain, **c** becomes the province, the craving between **b** and **c** is severed, and harmony is lost.

**Step 6: Object b succumbs**
*   **Action:** `(succumb b)`
*   **Time:** 00:05 - 00:06
*   **Description:** Object **b** succumbs to its pain. Following this, object **b** becomes a province and a planet, harmony is restored, and the pain in object **b** is removed.

**Step 7: Attack object a**
*   **Action:** `(attack a)`
*   **Time:** 00:06 - 00:07
*   **Description:** This action targets object **a**. Since **a** became a province and a planet in Step 2, and harmony was restored in Step 6, the attack can occur. As a result, object **a** is put into a state of pain, and it loses its status as a province and planet. Harmony is once again lost.

**Step 8: Overcome object a from object c**
*   **Action:** `(overcome a c)`
*   **Time:** 00:07 - 00:08
*   **Description:** Finally, object **a** overcomes object **c**. This requires object **c** to be the current province (achieved in Step 5) and object **a** to be in pain (achieved in Step 7). Completing this action restores harmony, makes object **a** a province, and establishes the goal: **object a now craves object c.**

PROBLEM

I am playing with a set of objects. Here are the actions I can do

   Attack object
   Feast object from another object
   Succumb object
   Overcome object from another object

I have the following restrictions on my actions:
    To perform Attack action, the following facts need to be true: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.
    Once Attack action is performed the following facts will be true: Pain object.
    Once Attack action is performed the following facts will be false: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.
    To perform Succumb action, the following facts need to be true: Pain object.
    Once Succumb action is performed the following facts will be true: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.    
    Once Succumb action is performed the following facts will be false: Pain object.
    To perform Overcome action, the following needs to be true: Province other object, Pain object.
    Once Overcome action is performed the following will be true: Harmony, Province object, Object Craves other object.
    Once Overcome action is performed the following will be false: Province other object, Pain object.
    To perform Feast action, the following needs to be true: Object Craves other object, Province object, Harmony.
    Once Feast action is performed the following will be true: Pain object, Province other object.
    Once Feast action is performed the following will be false:, Object Craves other object, Province object, Harmony.

My goal is to have that object a craves object b, object b craves object d and object d craves object c.

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1
------------------------------
To achieve the goal of having **object a** crave **object d** and **object b** crave **object c**, follow these sequence of actions:

1.  **00:00:00 - Feast object c from object b**: 
    Beginning with the initial state where harmony exists, object c craves object b, and object c is a province. Performing this action results in object c being in a state of pain, while object b becomes a province. Harmony is temporarily lost, and object c no longer craves object b.

2.  **00:01:00 - Succumb object c**: 
    With object c in pain, this action is performed to restore harmony. Object c is transformed back into a province and a planet, and its state of pain is removed.

3.  **00:02:00 - Feast object d from object a**: 
    With harmony restored, object d (which craves object a and is currently a province) feasts. This causes object d to enter a state of pain and turns object a into a province. Harmony is lost again, and object d no longer craves object a.

4.  **00:03:00 - Succumb object d**: 
    With object d in pain, succumbing allows object d to become a province and a planet once more, restoring harmony to the environment.

5.  **00:04:00 - Attack object a**: 
    Now that object a is both a province and a planet (following the feast in step 3) and harmony exists, object a is attacked. This puts object a into a state of pain and removes its province and planet status, as well as the state of harmony.

6.  **00:05:00 - Overcome object a from object d**: 
    Using the fact that object d is a province and object a is in pain, object a overcomes object d. This establishes that **object a now craves object d**, restores harmony, and makes object a a province.

7.  **00:06:00 - Attack object b**: 
    Since object b is a province (from the feast in step 1) and a planet, and harmony exists, object b is attacked. This results in object b feeling pain and losing its status as a province and planet, and harmony is lost.

8.  **00:07:00 - Overcome object b from object c**: 
    Finally, using the fact that object c is a province and object b is in pain, object b overcomes object c. This establishes that **object b now craves object c**, restores harmony, and concludes the process.
================================================================================

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1
------------------------------
Based on the provided plan and specification, here is the sequence of human-readable actions to achieve the goal:

**00:00 - Step 1: Feast object 'a' from object 'd'**
Initially, object 'a' craves object 'd', object 'a' is in its province, and harmony exists. Performing this action causes object 'a' to experience pain and shifts the province status to object 'd'. Consequently, the craving between 'a' and 'd' is satisfied, and the state of harmony is temporarily lost.

**00:01 - Step 2: Succumb object 'a'**
Since object 'a' is in pain from the previous feast, it must succumb. This action relieves the pain from 'a', reinstates 'a' as both a province and a planet, and restores the state of harmony.

**00:02 - Step 3: Attack object 'd'**
With 'd' now acting as a province (from Step 1), 'd' being a planet, and harmony restored (from Step 2), an attack is performed on object 'd'. This results in object 'd' experiencing pain. The province and planet status of 'd' are removed, and harmony is broken once more.

**00:03 - Step 4: Overcome object 'd' from object 'a'**
Object 'd' is currently in pain, and object 'a' is a province. By performing this action, object 'd' overcomes its pain using the province of 'a'. This restores harmony, makes 'd' a province, and establishes that **object 'd' now craves object 'a'**.

**00:04 - Step 5: Feast object 'b' from object 'c'**
Object 'b' craves object 'c', object 'b' is a province, and harmony exists. Upon feasting, object 'b' enters a state of pain, the province status moves to object 'c', and the craving for 'c' and the state of harmony are removed.

**00:05 - Step 6: Overcome object 'b' from object 'd'**
Object 'b' is in pain (from Step 5) and object 'd' is a province (from Step 4). By performing this action, object 'b' overcomes its pain using the province of 'd'. This restores harmony, makes 'b' a province, and establishes that **object 'b' now craves object 'd'**.

**Final Goal State:**
- Object 'b' craves object 'd'.
- Object 'd' craves object 'a'.
The goal is successfully reached.

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1
------------------------------
Based on the provided PDDL plan and specification, here is the sequence of actions to achieve the goal:

**Step 1: Feast object b from object d**
*   **Timestamp:** 00:00
*   **Action:** Initiate a "feast" action using object **b** and object **d**. 
*   **Result:** This action consumes the initial craving (b craves d), removes the province status from object b, and disrupts the state of harmony. Object b is now in a state of pain, and object d is established as a province.

**Step 2: Overcome object b from object c**
*   **Timestamp:** 00:01
*   **Action:** Perform the "overcome" action for object **b** from object **c**.
*   **Result:** Object b overcomes its state of pain, restoring harmony to the environment. Object b is reinstated as a province, and the first goal condition is met: **object b now craves object c**. Object c is no longer a province.

**Step 3: Attack object d**
*   **Timestamp:** 00:02
*   **Action:** Execute an "attack" on object **d**.
*   **Result:** Since object d was a province and a planet, and harmony was present, the attack is successful. Object d is now in a state of pain. Consequently, object d loses its province and planet status, and harmony is once again disrupted.

**Step 4: Overcome object d from object a**
*   **Timestamp:** 00:03
*   **Action:** Perform the final "overcome" action for object **d** from object **a**.
*   **Result:** Object d overcomes its state of pain, restoring harmony for the final time. Object d becomes a province, and the second goal condition is met: **object d now craves object a**. Object a is no longer a province.

**Final Goal Status:** 
- Object b craves object c: **Achieved**
- Object d craves object a: **Achieved**

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1
------------------------------
Based on the provided PDDL domain, problem, and plan, here is the sequence of human-readable actions to achieve the goal:

**Step 1: 00:00 - 00:01**
**Action: Feast object c from object b**
In this initial step, because object c craves object b, object c is in a province, and harmony exists, object c feasts from object b. This results in object c feeling pain and object b becoming a province, while harmony is lost and object c no longer craves object b.

**Step 2: 00:01 - 00:02**
**Action: Succumb object c**
Since object c is currently in pain, it undergoes the succumb action. This restores harmony, removes the pain from object c, and establishes object c as both a province and a planet.

**Step 3: 00:02 - 00:03**
**Action: Feast object d from object a**
With harmony restored and object d being in a province and craving object a, object d feasts from object a. This causes object d to feel pain and object a to become a province, while harmony and the craving between d and a are removed.

**Step 4: 00:03 - 00:04**
**Action: Succumb object d**
Object d, which was in pain from the previous step, now succumbs. This action restores harmony once again, removes the pain from object d, and establishes object d as a province and a planet.

**Step 5: 00:04 - 00:05**
**Action: Attack object a**
With harmony present and object a serving as both a province (from Step 3) and a planet (initially), object a is attacked. As a result, object a feels pain, and it is no longer a province, a planet, or in a state of harmony.

**Step 6: 00:05 - 00:06**
**Action: Overcome object a from object b**
Finally, object a (which is in pain) overcomes object b (which is a province). This restores harmony, makes object a a province, and achieves the final goal: **object a now craves object b**.

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1
------------------------------
Based on the provided PDDL domain, problem, and plan, here is the sequence of actions to achieve the goal:

**Plan Execution Sequence:**

*   **Step 1: 08:00 AM - Feast object a from object b.**
    Object a is currently the province and craves object b. By feasting, object a enters a state of pain, object b becomes the new province, and harmony is temporarily lost.
*   **Step 2: 08:01 AM - Succumb object a.**
    Object a is released from its state of pain. Consequently, object a becomes both a province and a planet, and harmony is restored to the system.
*   **Step 3: 08:02 AM - Feast object b from object c.**
    With object b as the province craving object c, this action puts object b into a state of pain. Object c now becomes the province, and harmony is once again lost.
*   **Step 4: 08:03 AM - Succumb object b.**
    Object b is released from pain. This restores harmony and establishes object b as a province and a planet.
*   **Step 5: 08:04 AM - Feast object c from object d.**
    Object c, the current province, feasts from object d. Object c enters a state of pain, object d becomes the new province, and harmony is lost.
*   **Step 6: 08:05 AM - Succumb object c.**
    Object c is released from pain, restoring harmony and making object c a province and a planet.
*   **Step 7: 08:06 AM - Attack object d.**
    Since object d is currently the province and a planet, it is attacked. This puts object d into a state of pain and removes the state of harmony.
*   **Step 8: 08:07 AM - Overcome object d from object a.**
    Object d (in pain) overcomes object a (the province). As a result, object d becomes the province and now craves object a. Harmony is restored.
*   **Step 9: 08:08 AM - Attack object c.**
    With harmony present and object c acting as a province/planet, object c is attacked. It enters a state of pain and harmony is lost.
*   **Step 10: 08:09 AM - Overcome object c from object d.**
    Object c overcomes object d. Object c becomes the province and now craves object d. Harmony is restored.
*   **Step 11: 08:10 AM - Attack object b.**
    Object b is attacked while it is a province and a planet. Object b enters a state of pain and harmony is lost.
*   **Step 12: 08:11 AM - Overcome object b from object c.**
    Finally, object b overcomes object c. Object b becomes the province and now craves object c. Harmony is restored.

**Final Goal State Reached:**
The system concludes with the following relationships:
- Object b craves object c.
- Object c craves object d.
- Object d craves object a.
